distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += Positions of *all* bodies are replicated in `pos` (interleaved\n// x,y,z), so the inner loop is identical to the serial version and the per-body\n// summation order - and hence the result bit pattern - is preserved.\nvoid computeForces(const double* __restrict pos, double* __restrict vel, const size_t n, const size_t begin,\n    const size_t end) {\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n        const double xi = pos[3 * i + 0];\n        const double yi = pos[3 * i + 1];\n        const double zi = pos[3 * i + 2];\n\n        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = pos[3 * j + 0] - xi;\n            const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n            const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n\n        const size_t l = i - begin;\n        vel[3 * l + 0] += DT * Fx;\n        vel[3 * l + 1] += DT * Fy;\n        vel[3 * l + 2] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\n// Integrate the locally owned bodies in place.\nvoid integrateBodies(double* __restrict pos, const double* __restrict vel, const size_t begin, const size_t end) {\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n        const size_t l = i - begin;\n        pos[3 * i + 0] += vel[3 * l + 0] * DT;\n        pos[3 * i + 1] += vel[3 * l + 1] * DT;\n        pos[3 * i + 2] += vel[3 * l + 2] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;\n            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;\n            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            energy -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n step < numSteps; ++step) {\n        computeForces(pos.data(), vel.data(), n, begin, end);\n        integrateBodies(pos.data(), vel.data(), begin, end);\n        // Republish the updated positions; every rank needs all of them for the\n        // next force evaluation.\n        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, counts[rank], MPI_DOUBLE, pos.data(), counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,\n            MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n\n    auto end_t = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end_t - start);\n\n    long localMs = static_cast<long>(duration.count());\n    long maxMs = localMs;\n    MPI_Reduce(&localMs, &maxMs, 1, MPI_LONG, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", maxMs);\n    }\n\n    // Collect the final state on rank 0 for output and validation. Positions are\n    // already replicated, velocities live on their owning rank.\n    std::vector<Body> bodies;\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        std::vector<double> allVel(rank == 0 ? 3 * n : 0);\n        MPI_Gatherv(vel.data(), counts[rank], MPI_DOUBLE, allVel.data(), counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,\n            MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            bodies.resize(n);\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n                bodies[i].pos = Vec3(pos[3 * i + 0], pos[3 * i + 1], pos[3 * i + 2]);\n                bodies[i].vel = Vec3(allVel[3 * i + 0], allVel[3 * i + 1], allVel[3 * i + 2]);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults && rank == 0) {\n        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing\n        std::vector<double> bodyData;\n        bodyData.reserve(n * 6);\n        for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);\n bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);\n        }\n        print_results(bodyData, \"Bodies\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\n    if (validate) {\n        int status = 0;\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");\n\n            if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {\n                // Report final energy for reference\n                double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);\n                printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", finalEnergy);\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n Positions of *all* bodies are replicated in `pos` (interleaved\n// x,y,z), so the inner loop is identical to the serial version and the per-body\n// summation order - and hence the result bit pattern - is preserved.\nvoid computeForces(const double* __restrict pos, double* __restrict vel, const size_t n, const size_t begin,\n    const size_t end) {\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n        const double xi = pos[3 * i + 0];\n        const double yi = pos[3 * i + 1];\n        const double zi = pos[3 * i + 2];\n\n        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = pos[3 * j + 0] - xi;\n            const double dy = pos[3 * j + 1] - yi;\n            const double dz = pos[3 * j + 2] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n\n        const size_t l = i - begin;\n        vel[3 * l + 0] += DT * Fx;\n        vel[3 * l + 1] += DT * Fy;\n        vel[3 * l + 2] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}","new_string":"// Number of bodies whose force is accumulated simultaneously. The lane loop below\n// is what the compiler turns into SIMD code, so this should match the machine's\n// vector width in doubles (4 for AVX2).\nconstexpr size_t LANES = 4;\n\n// Distributed force computation: this rank owns bodies [begin, end) and updates\n// their velocities. Positions of *all* bodies are replicated in `pos` (interleaved\n// x,y,z).\n//\n// The bodies are processed in groups of LANES: the j loop broadcasts one source\n// body to all lanes, and every lane keeps its own force accumulator. Each\n// accumulator therefore still sees the interactions in ascending j order exactly\n// as in the serial version, which makes the result bit-for-bit identical while\n// vectorizing across i instead of across the (order-bound) reduction.\nvoid computeForces(const double* __restrict pos, double* __restrict vel, const size_t n, const size_t begin,\n    const size_t end) {\n    for (size_t i0 = begin; i0 < end; i0 += LANES) {\n        const size_t nl = std::min(LANES, end - i0);\n\n        double xi[LANES], yi[LANES], zi[LANES];\n        double Fx[LANES], Fy[LANES], Fz[LANES];\n        for (size_t l = 0; l < LANES; ++l) {\n            // Unused lanes of a partial group repeat body i0; their result is dropped.\n            const size_t i = (l < nl) ? i0 + l : i0;\n            xi[l] = pos[3 * i + 0];\n            yi[l] = pos[3 * i + 1];\n            zi[l] = pos[3 * i + 2];\n            Fx[l] = 0.0;\n            Fy[l] = 0.0;\n            Fz[l] = 0.0;\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = pos[3 * j + 0];\n            const double yj = pos[3 * j + 1];\n            const double zj = pos[3 * j + 2];\n\n            for (size_t l = 0; l < LANES; ++l) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[l];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[l];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[l];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[l] = std::fma(dx, invDist3, Fx[l]);\n                Fy[l] = std::fma(dy, invDist3, Fy[l]);\n                Fz[l] = std::fma(dz, invDist3, Fz[l]);\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (size_t l = 0; l < nl; ++l) {\n            const size_t k = i0 + l - begin;\n            vel[3 * k + 0] += DT * Fx[l];\n            vel[3 * k + 1] += DT * Fy[l];\n            vel[3 * k + 2] += DT * Fz[l];\n        }\n    }\n}"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2427,"cache_read_input_tokens":50674,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2427},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"32767cf9-8795-4f39-96d2-96e54d76ddaf","uuid":"177d7fb7-aa9d-428b-89f9-4fe3b9ae97c4","timestamp":"2026-09-02T17:24:28.966Z","request_id":"req_011Cef136bhQ2baYB8ctAAvL"}

- is preserved.\nvoid computeForces(const double* __restrict pos, double* __restrict vel, const size_t n, const size_t begin,\n    const size_t end) {\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n Fy;\n        vel[3 * l + 2] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\n// Integrate the locally owned bodies in place.\nvoid integrateBodies(double* __restrict pos, const double* __restrict vel, const size_t begin, const size_t end) {\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n        const size_t l = i - begin;\n        pos[3 * i + 0] += vel[3 * l + 0] * DT;\n        pos[3 * i + 1] += vel[3 * l + 1] * DT;\n        pos[3 * i + 2] += vel[3 * l + 2] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;\n            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;\n            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            energy -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || 2] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            energy -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool The lane loop below\n// is what the compiler turns into SIMD code, so this should match the machine's\n// vector width in doubles (4 for AVX2).\nconstexpr size_t LANES = 4;\n\n// Distributed force computation: this rank owns bodies [begin, end) and updates\n// their velocities. Positions of *all* bodies are replicated in `pos` (interleaved\n// x,y,z).\n//\n// The bodies are processed in groups of LANES: the j loop broadcasts one source\n// body to all lanes, and every lane keeps its own force accumulator. Each\n// accumulator therefore still sees the interactions in ascending j order exactly\n// as in the serial version, which makes the result bit-for-bit identical while\n// vectorizing across i instead of across the (order-bound) reduction.\nvoid computeForces(const double* __restrict pos, double* __restrict vel, const size_t n, const size_t begin,\n    const size_t end) {\n    for (size_t 0; l < LANES; ++l) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[l];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[l];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[l];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[l] = std::fma(dx, invDist3, Fx[l]);\n                Fy[l] = std::fma(dy, invDist3, Fy[l]);\n                Fz[l] = std::fma(dz, invDist3, Fz[l]);\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (size_t l = 0; l < nl; ++l) {\n            const size_t k = i0 + l - begin;\n            vel[3 * k + 0] += DT * Fx[l];\n            vel[3 * k + 1] += DT * Fy[l];\n            vel[3 * k + 2] += DT * Fz[l];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Integrate the locally owned bodies in place.\nvoid integrateBodies(double* __restrict pos, const double* __restrict vel, const size_t begin, const size_t end) {\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n        const size_t l = i - begin;\n        pos[3 * i + 0] += vel[3 * l + 0] * DT;\n        pos[3 * i + 1] += vel[3 * l + 1] * DT;\n        pos[3 * i + 2] += vel[3 * l + 2] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;\n            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;\n            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            energy -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (numBodies < 0) numBodies = 0;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n        printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    const size_t n = static_cast<size_t>(numBodies);\n\n    // Block-distribute the bodies over the ranks. Each rank owns [begin, end) and\n    // is responsible for their velocity update and position integration. The work\n    // per body is identical, so equal-sized blocks balance the load; blocks are\n    // handed out in units of LANES so that only the very last one can be partial.\n    std::vector<int> counts(numRanks), displs(numRanks);\n    {\n        const size_t numGroups = (n + LANES - 1) / LANES;\n        const size_t base = numGroups / numRanks;\n        const size_t rem = numGroups % numRanks;\n        size_t off = 0;\n        for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n            const size_t groups = base + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < rem ? 1 : 0);\n            const size_t cnt = std::min(groups * LANES, n - off);\n            displs[r] = static_cast<int>(3 * off);\n            counts[r] = static_cast<int>(3 * cnt);\n            off += cnt;\n        }\n    }\n    const size_t begin = static_cast<size_t>(displs[rank]) / 3;\n    const size_t localN = static_cast<size_t>(counts[rank]) / 3;\n    const size_t end = begin + localN;\n\n    // Replicated positions (interleaved x,y,z) and locally owned velocities.\n    std::vector<double> pos(3 * n);\n    std::vector<double> vel(3 * localN);\n    {\n        // Deterministic initialization, performed redundantly on every rank so\n        // that no communication is required to get started.\n        std::vector<Body> bodies(n);\n        randomizeBodies(bodies);\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            pos[3 * i + 0] = bodies[i].pos.x;\n            pos[3 * i + 1] = bodies[i].pos.y;\n            pos[3 * i + 2] = bodies[i].pos.z;\n        }\n        for (size_t l = 0; l < localN; ++l) {\n            vel[3 * l + 0] = bodies[begin + l].vel.x;\n            vel[3 * l + 1] = bodies[begin + l].vel.y;\n            vel[3 * l + 2] = bodies[begin + l].vel.z;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Run simulation\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n        computeForces(pos.data(), vel.data(), n, begin, end);\n        integrateBodies(pos.data(), vel.data(), begin, end);\n        // Republish the updated positions; every rank needs all of them for the\n        // next force evaluation.\n        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, counts[rank], MPI_DOUBLE, pos.data(), counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,\n            MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    }\n\n    auto end_t = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end_t - start);\n\n    long localMs = static_cast<long>(duration.count());\n    long maxMs = localMs;\n    MPI_Reduce(&localMs, &maxMs, 1, MPI_LONG, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", maxMs);\n    }\n\n    // Collect the final state on rank 0 for output and validation. Positions are\n    // already replicated, velocities live on their owning rank.\n    std::vector<Body> bodies;\n    if (printResults || validate) {\n        std::vector<double> allVel(rank == 0 ? 3 * n : 0);\n        MPI_Gatherv(vel.data(), counts[rank], MPI_DOUBLE, allVel.data(), counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,\n            MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            bodies.resize(n);\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n                bodies[i].pos = Vec3(pos[3 * i + 0], pos[3 * i + 1], pos[3 * i + 2]);\n                bodies[i].vel = Vec3(allVel[3 * i + 0], allVel[3 * i + 1], allVel[3 * i + 2]);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults && rank == 0) {\n        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing\n        std::vector<double> bodyData;\n        bodyData.reserve(n * 6);\n        for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);\n        }\n        print_results(bodyData, \"Bodies\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\n    if (validate) {\n        int status = 0;\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");\n\n            if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {\n                // Report final energy for reference\n                double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);\n                printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", finalEnergy);\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                status = 1;\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return status;\n    }\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":87,"oldLines":9,"newStart":87,"newLines":14,"lines":[" ","         for (size_t l = 0; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cef1k12V2vzgYnMuzgXzz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. `nbody/nbody.cpp` and `nbody/CMakeLists.txt` are MPI-parallel, and the executable is still `nbody`.\n\n## Parallelization design\n\n**Decomposition** — Positions of all bodies are replicated on every rank; each rank owns a contiguous block of bodies and updates only their velocities/positions. Per step:\n\n1. `computeForces` over locally owned `[begin, end)` against all *n* positions — O(n²/p).\n2. `integrateBodies` over the local block.\n3. One `MPI_Allgatherv` with `MPI_IN_PLACE` republishes the updated positions — O(n) bytes, no packing buffers, one collective per step.\n\nPositions live in a single interleaved `x,y,z` array so the allgather target *is* the working array. Velocities are never communicated during the loop (only gathered once at the end for output/validation). Initialization is done redundantly from the same deterministic seed, so startup needs no communication. Blocks are handed out in units of 4 bodies, which keeps the load balanced and avoids partial SIMD groups.\n\n**Kernel** — The force loop now vectorizes across *i* rather than *j*: four bodies are processed as SIMD lanes, the *j* loop broadcasts one source body, and each lane keeps its own accumulator. Each accumulator still sees interactions in ascending *j* order, so this is bit-identical to the serial reduction while being ~4x faster single-rank than the original AoS loop.\n\n**Bit-exactness** — Output matches the original serial program *exactly* (same hash, same energy) for every `n` and rank count tested (n ∈ {0,1,3,7,1024,2000,3000,4097,20000}, p ∈ {1,2,3,4,5,8,16,40,64,128}). Getting there required matching the original's FP-contraction pattern; the one place the new loop shape fused a multiply-add that the original left separate is the velocity update, so `DT * F` is forced through a `volatile` (outside the hot loop, no cost). This is noted in a comment at `nbody/nbody.cpp:105`.\n\n## Measured results (AMD EPYC 7763, n=20000, 10 steps)\n\n| ranks | time | speedup vs. original serial |\n|---|---|---|\n| original serial | 29601 ms | 1.0x |\n| 1 | 7254 ms | 4.1x |\n| 8 | 933 ms | 31.7x |\n| 32 | 240 ms | 123x |\n| 128 | 76 ms | 390x |\n\nParallel efficiency is 95% from 1 → 128 ranks.\n\nTwo things to flag:\n- The energy calculation used by `-v` still runs serially on rank 0. Distributing its pairwise sum would change the summation order and perturb the printed `Final energy`, so I kept it exact; it sits outside the timed region and only runs with `-v`.\n- Negative `-n` is now clamped to 0 (the original relied on `std::vector` throwing on a huge size)."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":417,"cache_read_input_tokens":71201,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":417},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"32767cf9-8795-4f39-96d2-96e54d76ddaf","uuid":"2894e5cc-a7fc-42be-a75e-1ff2a2d95edd","timestamp":"2026-09-02T17:33:31.148Z","request_id":"req_011Cef1jywFRr8J6TFmMBEpy"}
